- **git-drill**: Practice git hands-on in a safe throwaway sandbox or create a reusable drill scenario spec. Manufactures realistic scenarios (first commit, merge conflict, detached HEAD, wrong branch, bad reset) in a disposable repo, then coaches fixing them with hints, solution on request, and retry.
